				localization_key = unlock_ROC_policy_card_tt

				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = ROC_deny_the_enemy
				}
				every_navy_leader = {
					add_trait = { trait = green_water_expert } 
				}
			}
			ELSE_IF = {
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = ROC_dominate_the_enemy
				}
				every_navy_leader = {
					add_trait = { trait = blue_water_expert }
				}
			}
			ELSE = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = ROC_promote_admiral_tt
			}
		}

				}
			}
			else = {
				random_core_state = {

						is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
						is_coastal = yes 
						air_facility < 1
						land_facility < 1
						naval_facility < 1
						nuclear_facility < 1
					}
					construct_building_in_random_province = {
						naval_facility = 1
					}
				}
			}
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = ROC_strike_from_below
				}
				generate_scientist_character = {
					portrait_tag_override = ROC
					traits = { scientist_trait_submarine_specialist }
					skills = {
						specialization_naval = 2
					}
				}
			}

				limit = {

				}

			}

			IF = { 
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = ROC_projecting_power
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = ROC_modify_ROCAF_tt
				add_ideas = { CHI_appropriate_design_offensive_idea }
			}
			ELSE_IF = { 
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = ROC_controlling_our_skies
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = ROC_modify_ROCAF_tt
				add_ideas = { CHI_appropriate_design_defensive_idea }
			}
			ELSE = {
				
				custom_effect_tooltip = ROC_an_appropriate_design_3_tt
				custom_effect_tooltip = ROC_modify_ROCAF_tt
				effect_tooltip = {
					add_ideas = { CHI_appropriate_design_offensive_idea }
				}
				

				custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt

				custom_effect_tooltip = ROC_an_appropriate_design_2_tt
				custom_effect_tooltip = ROC_modify_ROCAF_tt

				}

		}
		completion_reward = {

			}

				limit = {
					has_dlc = "Arms Against Tyranny"
				}
				mio:CHI_camco_organization = {
					add_mio_size = 2
					add_mio_funds_gain_factor = 0.25
					add_mio_research_bonus = 0.05
				}
				mio:CHI_camco_fighter_organization = {
					add_mio_size = 2
					add_mio_funds_gain_factor = 0.25
					add_mio_research_bonus = 0.05
				}
				mio:CHI_camco_bomber_organization = {
					add_mio_size = 2
					add_mio_funds_gain_factor = 0.25
					add_mio_research_bonus = 0.05
				}
				mio:CHI_camco_3 = {
					add_mio_size = 2
					add_mio_funds_gain_factor = 0.25
					add_mio_research_bonus = 0.05
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
				if = {
					limit = {
						596 = { # Zhejiang
							is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
							free_building_slots = {
								building = arms_factory
								size > 1
								include_locked = yes
							}
						}
					}
					596 = {
						add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
						add_building_construction = {
							type = arms_factory
							level = 2
							instant_build = yes
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					every_core_state = {
						limit = {
							is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
							free_building_slots = {
								building = arms_factory
								size > 1
								include_locked = yes
							}
						}
						random_select_amount = 2
						add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
						add_building_construction = {
							type = arms_factory
							level = 1
							instant_build = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			ELSE = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = available_designer
				show_ideas_tooltip = CHI_camco_SEA
				custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
				if = {
					limit = {
						596 = { # Zhejiang
							is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
							free_building_slots = {
								building = arms_factory
								size > 1
								include_locked = yes
							}
						}
					}
					596 = {
						add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
						add_building_construction = {
							type = arms_factory
							level = 2
							instant_build = yes
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					every_core_state = {
						limit = {
							is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
							free_building_slots = {
								building = arms_factory
								size > 1
								include_locked = yes
							}
						}

	shared_focus = {   ##启动天空计划
		id = ROC_initiate_the_sky_projects
		icon = GFX_focus_rocketry 
		prerequisite = { 
			focus = ROC_officialize_the_rocaf 
		}
		x = 0
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = ROC_officialize_the_rocaf

		cost = 5

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 4
		}

		available = {
		}

		bypass = {

		}

		cancel_if_invalid = yes
		continue_if_invalid = no
		available_if_capitulated = no

		complete_tooltip = {

		}

		#search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_RESEARCH }
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10 
		}
		completion_reward = {
			add_to_variable = {
				var = adorable_heart_focus_var
				value = 0.5
				tooltip = adorable_heart_focus_var_change_tt
			}
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = CHI_initiate_the_sky_projects
				bonus = 0.4
				uses = 1
				category = rocketry
			}

			add_breakthrough_progress = {
				specialization = specialization_air
				value = 0.75
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			add_victory_points = {
				province = 8102
				value = 1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					348 = { # Jiuquan
						is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
						air_facility < 1
						land_facility < 1
						naval_facility < 1
						nuclear_facility < 1
					}
				}
				348 = {
					add_building_construction = {
						type = air_facility
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
						province = { id = 8102 }
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_core_state = {
					limit = {
						is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
						air_facility < 1
						land_facility < 1
						naval_facility < 1
						nuclear_facility < 1
					} 
					add_building_construction = {
						type = air_facility
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
						province = {
							all_provinces = yes
							first = yes #just picks the first valid after reduction
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			add_to_variable = { 
				CHI_ROCAF_sp_tag_rocket_speed_factor = 0.10
				tooltip = sp_tag_rocket_speed_factor_tt
			}
		}
	}
